- **qd-belenos-bdsc-diagnostic-readout** (exploratory reanalysis, hardware). The readout of the four completed jobs, by the preregistered rule, of the crossed BDSC diagnostic on qpu:belenos (96 instances: six target-complement states x two templates x four QRNG seeds x two blocks, 40000 samples per iteration with 39959 to 40000 eligible one-click trials, empty mitigation) selects the stationarity branch: matched requests changed between the blocks beyond their class means (heterogeneity statistic 553 on 45 degrees of freedom) while all 48 matched pairs returned identical compiled circuits and no instance leaked. Outside the branch rule, the support-conditioned rerun finds the matched changes homogeneous about their class means (45.8 on 45), so the instance-specific change lives in the aggregate fraction of clicks outside each setting's target support, while the superpositions keep a common on-support shift of -0.0048 (z -7.7); the g basis state shifted by +0.0035 (z 11.6); the seed effect on the superpositions is state-specific in both readings and stays so among seed slots that share a job (2406 on 24 raw, 1941 on 24 support-conditioned); and all 32 sum-rule residuals lie beyond the 0.02 margin, between -0.14 and -0.24.
  Boundary: One device, two blocks a day apart, read descriptively: block 2 was resubmitted past a stop condition with no amendment retained before it (the runbook's protocol deviation of 2026-10-23). Standard errors and p-values are reference values under independent Bernoulli trials, uncalibrated while the provider contract leaves count provenance and uncorrected observations unresolved. Identical returned circuits show one nominal model; attributing the change to the device rather than the compiler holds only if a returned circuit describes the configuration that ran, which the contract leaves unresolved. The branch names the next amendment, stationarity and provider calibration before any fixed-effect confirmation, which reruns under that amendment; it makes no certification decision, and physical_perf ratios stay an uninterpreted provider diagnostic.
  Evidence: [bdsc_diagnostic_readout-runner-image.json](../../results/quandela/bdsc_diagnostic_readout-runner-image.json), [bdsc_diagnostic_readout.json](../../results/quandela/bdsc_diagnostic_readout.json), [bdsc-qualification-case.md](../runbooks/bdsc-qualification-case.md), [bdsc_diagnostic.py](../../scripts/bdsc_diagnostic.py).
  Gates: replayed by core-647.
  Acquisitions: qd-qpu-belenos-bdsc-diagnostic-block1-job1, qd-qpu-belenos-bdsc-diagnostic-block1-job2, qd-qpu-belenos-bdsc-diagnostic-block2-job1, qd-qpu-belenos-bdsc-diagnostic-block2-job2.
- **qd-belenos-bdsc-stray-mode-veto** (exploratory reanalysis, hardware). Vetoing output mode 5, which lies outside every selection and target support of the crossed BDSC diagnostic on qpu:belenos and whose plan-time dark-count rate in every run receipt (3137.7 per second before block 1, 1847.5 before block 2) is the largest of any mode, removes the instance-specific block change of the four completed diagnostic jobs: the heterogeneity of matched requests about their class means falls from 553 to 58.0 on 45 degrees of freedom (p 0.09) whether the mode-5 one-click samples leave the eligible count or the mode-5 detector is removed from every pattern. Across the 48 matched instances the mode-5 share changed by -0.0069 with standard deviation 0.0119, while every other off-support mode together changed by +0.0002 with standard deviation 0.0026. Under the veto the g basis state's block shift reads -0.0007 (z -3.6) instead of +0.0035, the superpositions keep a common shift of -0.0044 (z -7.2), the seed effects stay resolved, and the mode-5 share falls with position in two of the four jobs (rank correlation -0.49 and -0.47, p 0.016 and 0.020). The block-mean mode-5 share fell from 0.0144 to 0.0075, a ratio of 0.52 against the plan-time dark-rate ratio of 0.59, and the coincident trials (one mode-5 click beside exactly one other click) carry the same selected fraction as the other eligible trials (47.7 over 66 instances, conditional hypergeometric p 0.40).
  Boundary: The veto was chosen after the diagnostic's outcomes were seen, on runs that are themselves read descriptively (the runbook's protocol deviation of 2026-10-23), so it names a hypothesis that the stationarity amendment of the qualification runbook tests on new data and confirms nothing. Standard errors are reference values under independent Bernoulli trials. The dark-count rate is a plan-time platform reading, not an acquisition-time measurement: the two block-1 jobs share one reading yet their mean mode-5 shares differ (0.0123 and 0.0165), and physical_perf supplies no confirmed acquisition clock (docs/notes/quandela/strategy.md), so the ranks of the share against dark rate / physical_perf are a conditional diagnostic and the dark-click mechanism stays a candidate.
  Evidence: [bdsc_stray_mode_veto.json](../../results/quandela/bdsc_stray_mode_veto.json), [bdsc_stray_mode_veto-runner-image.json](../../results/quandela/bdsc_stray_mode_veto-runner-image.json), [bdsc-qualification-case.md](../runbooks/bdsc-qualification-case.md), [bdsc_stray_mode_veto.py](../../scripts/bdsc_stray_mode_veto.py).
  Gates: replayed by core-667.
  Acquisitions: qd-qpu-belenos-bdsc-diagnostic-block1-job1, qd-qpu-belenos-bdsc-diagnostic-block1-job2, qd-qpu-belenos-bdsc-diagnostic-block2-job1, qd-qpu-belenos-bdsc-diagnostic-block2-job2.
  Depends on: qd-belenos-bdsc-diagnostic-readout.

A post hoc adjudication of the Tuna-17 kernel cancellation series, chosen after the bright control falsified H2: the readout-corrected kernel-minus-bright difference at phi = pi/36 reads -0.0056 +- 0.0058 (z -0.97), so the zero-divisor sign stays unresolved on this device once the kernel-independent signed response is subtracted.

  Assumptions: the two batches of a contrast carry the same within-batch position effect, so the +, -, -, + balance across them cancels it; a batch's readout assignment factor equals its batch pair's, whose two references sit in different batches; the spread of the series' readout references enters the corrected errors.

## Open claims

- **a5-stiefel-universal** (mathematical claim, algebra). Moreno Section 5 asks whether every A_5 Stiefel element (a, b), with pure orthogonal equal-norm entries in A_4, is a zero divisor; the universal A_5 question remains open.
  Boundary: The 32 exact rational points and the 96 modulo-p identity-test points support it and prove nothing beyond themselves; by the Pfaffian reduction any point where Pf(L_a + i R_b) is nonzero refutes it and entails an open dense set of such points, and a proof needs Pf(L_a + i R_b) to vanish on the whole Stiefel set.
  Proof origin: unproved-open.
  Assumptions: cd_core.cd_sign doubling convention (a, b)(c, d) = (ac - conj(d) b, da + b conj(c)).
  Evidence: [math-0512517.md](../notes/math-0512517.md).
  Depends on: a5-stiefel-selected-nullity, a5-stiefel-pfaffian-reduction, a5-stiefel-pfaffian-identity-test.
- **dimension32-global-topology** (mathematical claim, geometry). A classification of the complete dimension-32 two-sided rank strata: components, smooth strata, closure incidences, attachments and integral invariants.
  Boundary: Restricted models above supply fixtures and falsifiers; none covers the unrestricted pair space.
  Proof origin: unproved-open.
  Assumptions: cd_core.cd_sign doubling convention (a, b)(c, d) = (ac - conj(d) b, da + b conj(c)).
  Evidence: [RESEARCH_ROADMAP.md](../RESEARCH_ROADMAP.md), [rank_locus_two_dimension_gap.md](../notes/rank_locus_two_dimension_gap.md).
  Depends on: four-support-topology, principal-skew-atlas, kernel-limit-slice, nullity-twelve-cone.
- **a5-nonzero-divisor-two-dimension-gap** (mathematical claim, geometry). At every certified witness of an A_5 rank-locus family with a non-zero-divisor entry ({a, 0} at nullity 12, b in W(a) at nullity 8, the two nullity-4 families), D_(5,k) has local dimension equal to its tangent bound, two above the family, with the extra directions leaving H_5^perp through e_8 and e_24.
  Boundary: The certificate bounds the local dimension between the family dimension and the tangent bound, and the tangent bound is 15 at three further {a, 0} points, two with non-parallel halves, as at the witnesses. The second- and third-order compatibility tests are vacuous at all 12 witnesses (every velocity in T_F + span{e_8, e_24} passes both), and exact half-rotation curves carry e_8 and e_24 at the nonzd-axis and nonzd-nonzd-locus witnesses only. At nullity 12 the composite of both half turns over the {a, 0} family leaves D_(5,12) (rank 32 at an exact rational point), and one turn drops to nullity 4 from generic family points, so that candidate piece fails while the local dimension stays undecided. Curves bound no local dimension from below, so a certified piece of the larger dimension through a witness would establish the statement and equations cutting out D_(5,k) below the tangent bound near a witness would refute it. At the nullity-12 witnesses, claim a5-nullity12-gap-extended-precision finds near-rank-20 points off the family that persist at 110 digits, which bound nothing because closeness to a rank-20 matrix places no exact locus point nearby. Claim a5-tangent-bound-not-linear shows exactly that T(x) is never inside the closed rank cone R_k, so the cone over a piece of the tangent-bound dimension through a witness would be curved. Claim a5-schur-germ-unobstructed finds the excess Schur-complement equations vanishing through order 63 on the smooth germ of the tangent-bound dimension at all 12 witnesses, so the statement there is equivalent to their exact vanishing on that germ, which an ideal-membership certificate or an explicit parameterization of the germ would establish. Claim a5-nullity12-quadric-germ establishes the statement for the {a, 0} family at its three nonzd-axis witnesses: the germ of R_12 there is the quadric cone V, of local dimension 15 on the unit sphere with e_8 and e_24 tangent, so the nullity-8 and nullity-4 families carry the open part.
  Proof origin: unproved-open.
  Assumptions: cd_core.cd_sign doubling convention (a, b)(c, d) = (ac - conj(d) b, da + b conj(c)).
  Evidence: [result.json](../evidence/rank_locus_second_order/result.json), [result.json](../evidence/rank_locus_rotation_composite/result.json), [audit.json](../evidence/rank_locus_rotation_composite/audit.json), [rank_locus_two_dimension_gap.md](../notes/rank_locus_two_dimension_gap.md), [README.md](../evidence/rank_locus_local_dimension/README.md), [README.md](../evidence/rank_locus_second_order/README.md), [README.md](../evidence/rank_locus_rotation_composite/README.md).
  Gates: replayed by core-553, core-585, core-586; replay failure paths checked by core-554.
  Depends on: a5-rank-locus-local-dimension, nullity-twelve-cone.

## Proposed claims

- **proposed-census-frequency** (proposed experiment, algebra). Read a per-triple associator witness on the 3360 sedenion triples with i and j nonzero and unequal: P(c = 1) = (1 - V+)/2 + f (V+ + V-)/2 gives their dark fraction f = 1848/3360, where pooled bright (omega = +1) and dark (omega = -1) references fix V+ and V-; the other 736 triples are bright exactly, so the census is f x 3360/4096 = 231/512, a test of how faithfully hardware carries the sign function.
  Boundary: Tests implementation fidelity, not the census theorem, which is proved. Under the Kingston noise model the factorized witness compiles to about 434 CZ with V+ = 0.370 and V- = 0.346, so a single visibility misses the census by 0.005 and single-pair references move it by +-0.02; the hardware visibility and shot cost stay unmeasured.
  Evidence: [result.json](../evidence/census_frequency_design/result.json), [README.md](../evidence/census_frequency_design/README.md).
  Gates: replayed by core-288.
  Depends on: cd-associator-census.
- **proposed-signed-kernel-probe** (proposed experiment, implementation). Perturb the certified kernel u = (e4 - e15)/sqrt2 of L_(e1+e10) by t L_e2: the dark probability t^2/(2+|t|)^2 is even in t, while the Hermitian dilation reference reads Re<v|B(t)|u> = t/(2+|t|), signed and first order.
  Boundary: Priced against direct counting before any hardware case; no universal speedup follows. The signed overlap holds for every unit vector u (signed-overlap-kernel-independence); only the dark probability depends on u lying in the kernel.
  Depends on: algebra-circuit-fixtures.
- **proposed-algebra-device-controlled-extensions** (proposed experiment, implementation). Operational case contracts pair algebra-specific response, singular-sector dynamics, coherent selection, sequential measurement and effective nonunitarity with property-removing mutations, unconditional accounting and prospective calibration controls.
  Boundary: Logical instruments and exact models are available. Fresh physical scheduling, block-level covariance and power, provider-specific costs and separate approval precede acquisition; squeezing, protected topology and quantum-code benefit remain capability- or definition-gated.
  Evidence: [hypercomplex-operator-evidence.md](../design/hypercomplex-operator-evidence.md).
  Gates: replay failure paths checked by core-671.
  Depends on: algebra-device-operational-falsifiers, algebra-device-logical-instrument-bank.
- **proposed-tuna17-zero-divisor-matched-response** (proposed experiment, hardware). Nine unsubmitted Tuna-17 jobs compare dark, bright and same-support quadrature witnesses with fixed routed shapes per region; the preregistered contrast cancels batch intercepts and a fixed region offset under a common affine response.
  Boundary: Local exact and compiler checks establish software conformance. Physical scheduling, refreshed budget, frozen covariance analysis and user approval precede provider execution; the contrast cannot identify a unique noise mechanism.
  Evidence: [tuna17-zero-divisor-matched-response.md](../design/tuna17-zero-divisor-matched-response.md).
  Gates: replayed by core-632, core-633.
- **proposed-conditional-ramsey-hamiltonian** (proposed experiment, hardware). Add Ramsey of qubit A conditioned on B in |0> and |1> to the Bell odd and even coherence rates: the design matrix for H/hbar = (omega_A Z_A + omega_B Z_B + J Z_A Z_B)/2 rises from rank 2 to 3, J = (nu_A|0 - nu_A|1)/2, and nu_odd + nu_even = nu_A|0 + nu_A|1 is the falsifier of the reduced diagonal model.
  Boundary: Each rate needs resolved quadratures, qualified exposure, phase-reference controls and delay diversity against winding. The ibm_kingston pair cases (68, 67) and (67, 66) are retired without a run (docs/state/QPU_RUN_CENSUS.md). The Tuna-17 coupler map (qi-tuna17-coupler-zz-map) runs the conditional-arm fit for J and omega on another device; no run collects the Bell odd and even coherence rates or the nu_odd + nu_even falsifier.
  Depends on: hw-coherence-window.
  Supersedes: withdrawn-interleaving-verified.
- **proposed-crossed-tile-template** (proposed experiment, hardware). Cross {affected template, reference template} x {candidate weak tile, reference tile}, optionally x {isolated, packed}, to separate tile, template and interaction effects on the weak-lane loss.
  Boundary: Tiles were chosen after seeing the data, so only prospective validation counts; qubit labels on different processors do not identify one defect.
  Depends on: hw-corner-explains-weak-lane.
- **proposed-first-bit-readout-controls** (proposed experiment, hardware). Read qubit 79 prepared in |1> immediately and after 4000 and 12500 dt idles (readout_delay in gororoba_quantum/diagnostic_controls.py), and add a repeated readout, to separate preparation, relaxation and measurement back-action behind the first-bit correlation.
  Boundary: Falsifier: one symmetric assignment model reproduces both labels' strata within their binomial intervals.
  Depends on: hw-first-bit-correlation.
- **proposed-echo-matched-dilation** (proposed experiment, hardware). Compare U Z U^dagger Z U against U U^dagger U at equal non-virtual gates and duration, with a per-edge CZ-phase reference whose X-quadrature arms calibrate its visibility and common phase, and an incoherent control on the same compile, to measure the error the dark probe carries and bound its conditional-phase share under the uniform-phase model.
  Boundary: Every physical coherent channel and extra depolarizing move the pair the same way under the retained noise models (echo-pair-coherent-sign), edge phases act jointly, and mixtures cancel, so the pair alone separates no rotation error from a CZ phase and leaves the residual unidentified without an incoherent control; the case binds the retained device compile.
  Depends on: echo-pair-coherent-sign, singular-sector-split.
- **proposed-scaffold-ab** (proposed experiment, hardware). Run the walk template bound to readout0 and readout1 beside measure-only and X-then-measure circuits on one tile under one calibration, isolating the scaffold's loss.
  Boundary: Design in gororoba_quantum/diagnostic_controls.py; needs its own written case.
  Depends on: hw-walk-scaffold-floor.
- **proposed-tuna17-q0-q6-double-x-discriminator** (proposed experiment, hardware). P1 after X X with no wait, beside a readout-0 and readout-1 pair in one Tuna-17 batch, separates an under-rotated X from |1> misassignment on Q0 and Q6: under-rotation by the angle their zero-wait P(1|1) implies predicts 0.98 and 0.52, misassignment 0.038 and 0.013. Ramsey inverted with the matched calibration cannot separate them when X and X90 share the under-rotation, because the raw quadratures then take the readout-scale form sin^2(beta) (1 + cos phi) / 2; a fault confined to native X keeps the X90 Ramsey at the earlier scale, which Q6's raw quadrature mean (0.064 against a floor of 0.148) excludes and Q0's (0.287 against 0.175) does not.
  Boundary: The predictions use the 05:26-05:33Z state; Tuna recalibrates between user jobs, so a run first checks that its readout pair still shows the fault, and leakage, whose X train plateaus instead of oscillating, needs X trains of three and more.
  Evidence: [experiments.md](../notes/quantum_inspire/experiments.md).
  Depends on: qi-tuna17-q0-q6-loss-not-readout-relaxation.
- **qi-tuna17-effective-idle-zz-map** (proposed experiment, hardware). A proposed confirmatory cross-check of the retained exploratory coupler map uses a gate-level conditional-Ramsey case covering all 24 edges of the retained Tuna-17 coupling graph in eight induced matchings, with both probe directions, static neighbor states 0 and 1, X/Y quadratures, six identical physical delay values including zero, and full four-state joint readout calibration. The 384 Ramsey and 32 calibration circuits form 139 batches at 8192 shots each (3407872 job shots), with no CZ gates. A source-bound analyzer reconstructs omega_A, omega_B and J, checks signal quality and reciprocal-J agreement, and uses Holm correction over 24 edge tests. A separately budgeted compact QX preflight contains the same 416 circuits and 139 batches on 2 to 8 qubits at 1024 shots each (425984 emulator job shots), with fixed ideal-output criteria. Neither case has been submitted. The hardware pause ladder puts its interbatch pauses between 4140 seconds at the 30-second first step and 16560 seconds at the 120-second ceiling. It is retained as a complete offline design reference and needs a revised delay/calibration acquisition plan before submission.
  Boundary: No acquisition or new spending authorization. Hardware batches contain at most three circuits and are acquired sequentially under the current repository policy; a compact QX preflight has its own proposed case and budget. Results would be effective static edge interactions in the declared parallel context, not reconstructed coupler flux or unique causal attribution. The Q1/Q2 nonedge and driven crosstalk require additional controls. All six delays must pass signal and phase guards; failed precision is inconclusive, not a null. Rates are conditional on the first-interval absolute range below 1 MHz, since the commensurate grid cannot identify all out-of-range aliases. The two probe directions are separate settings, not a simultaneous two-equatorial-qubit Ramsey experiment. Primitive timing sums are planning scenarios, not billed QPU time. Joint readout correction assumes assignment-channel stability across the calibration and Ramsey batches; the estimator does not remove between-batch drift. QX success would support ideal marginal gate and wiring conformance only. The retained exploratory map already found low coherence on some probes at 2 us; requiring signal at all six delays through 40 us can therefore yield inconclusive edges. Its results do not relax the prospective guards. The 139-batch version is not recommended for unchanged acquisition after the retained coherence and calibration changes; no shorter pilot is generated or authorized here. A future revision must set its own prospective thresholds rather than relax this estimator after observing data.
  Evidence: [plan.json](../../results/quantum_inspire/cases/zz_map_tuna_17/plan.json), [plan.json](../../results/quantum_inspire/cases/zz_map_qx/plan.json), [tuna_zz_map.md](../notes/quantum_inspire/tuna_zz_map.md), [tuna_zz_qx_preflight.md](../notes/quantum_inspire/tuna_zz_qx_preflight.md).
  Gates: source regenerated by core-594, core-596; replay failure paths checked by core-595.
  Depends on: qi-tuna17-zero-divisor-common-response, hw-idle-detuning-scale, qi-tuna17-coupler-zz-map.

## Superseded claims

- **hw-first-bit-disagreement** (exploratory reanalysis, hardware). About 11 percent of correction shots record the first bit as 0 for odd labels, and within that stratum the arm ordering reverses by label (s3 favors the known label, s1 the measured bit).

  Superseded by: hw-first-bit-correlation.
- **hw-fez-walk-control-discrepancy** (exploratory reanalysis, hardware). The union-template walk readout controls read 0.064/0.046 (lane 0) and 0.131/0.126 (lane 1) on ibm_fez against 0.540/0.440 and 0.587/0.521 in calibration-bound replay.
  Boundary: The controls exercise the compiled union template, so readout assignment alone does not explain the gap; the mechanism is unresolved.
  Evidence: [collect.json](../../results/live/2026-10-13T025444.603480Z/collect.json), [CAMPAIGN_FINDINGS.md](../../results/live/CAMPAIGN_FINDINGS.md).
  Acquisitions: fez-ripple-atlas.
  Superseded by: hw-walk-scaffold-floor.
- **proposed-qd-belenos-seeded-single-job-q1-mesh** (proposed experiment, hardware). Q1's case as one qpu:belenos job at compilation_seed 20 and 10000 samples (case belenos_mesh/job-13, about 1.01 credits) separates the unseeded single-job path from the single-job form at Q1's budget: void unless it serves 29dc6ef8; in_block inside source 20's light cone of at least 0.95 reads that the leak follows the unseeded path, at most 0.65 that it needs the single-job form at Q1's budget on 29dc6ef8 whatever the seed, and between reads intermediate.
  Boundary: One job on one mesh; whether an unseeded single job splits its budget across compilations or applies the mesh differently stays open after either reading. A run needs the user's approval of the written case.
  Evidence: [strategy.md](../notes/quandela/strategy.md).
  Depends on: qd-belenos-light-cone-reading.
  Superseded by: qd-belenos-q1-leak-single-job-form-whatever-seed.
- **proposed-qd-belenos-payload-class-series** (proposed experiment, hardware). Q1's case on qpu:belenos at compilation_seed 20 and 10000 samples per entry, first as a ComputationIterator holding one entry (case belenos_mesh/job-14), then job-15 (a single computation, the time control) after a job-14 pass or job-16 (two identical entries) after a job-14 fail, at most two jobs and about 2.03 credits, reads each entry's in-cone TVD from the served mesh's ideal: at most 0.20 passes, at least 0.50 fails, void off 29dc6ef8 or when the result's results_list length differs from the payload sent; job-14 pass with job-15 fail reads that the failure follows the single-computation payload class.
  Boundary: One mesh at one budget; job-14 always precedes its follower, so request form, entry position and elapsed execution stay coupled within the block, and each reading describes the ordered block, not a subsystem. Mitigation-off and native samples arms change the execution path and stay out. job-14 ran and failed (qd-belenos-singleton-iterator-fails-on-q1-mesh), so job-16 is the prescribed follower; a run needs the user's approval of the written case.
  Evidence: [strategy.md](../notes/quandela/strategy.md).
  Depends on: qd-belenos-single-job-failure-is-a-transformation.
  Superseded by: qd-belenos-q1-failure-persists-until-mesh-load.
- **iqm-garnet-signed-kernel-probe** (preregistered hardware inference, hardware). On IQM Garnet (calibration set 9069c4ef, 199 CZ on QB6, QB7, QB10, QB11, QB12, QB16 and QB17, one active-reset cycle with all-zeros heralding keeping 55.9 % of shots), the structure-matched signed probe of the certified sedenion kernel u = (e4 - e15)/sqrt2 of L(e1+e10) read the odd part (Z(t) - Z(-t))/2 of its control at +0.0220 +- 0.0067 for |t| = 0.25 (z 3.29, the sign of t resolved) and +0.0058 +- 0.0030 for |t| = 0.05 (z 1.94, unresolved), so the dilation overlap Re<v|B(t)|u> = t/(2 + |t|) is not falsified and the device carries its sign at |t| = 0.25. Both odd parts fit one visibility, V = 0.206 +- 0.054 times t/(2 + |t|) (chi-square 0.08, 1 dof), and the even offsets read -0.022 and -0.012. The jobs charged 4.50 credits.
  Boundary: One compile, one calibration set and two values of |t|; the |t| = 0.05 arm is underpowered, not a confirmation. The calibration-built simulator (depolarizing gates, readout errors, ALAP idle T1/T2) predicted odd parts +0.0296 and +0.0188 and even offsets near +0.029: the small-|t| odd part and the sign of the offsets depart from it, so its non-unital relaxation term does not describe this device. Heralding removes the active-reset excitation measured by iqm-garnet-reset-billing-pilot; it does not test the dark-probe law t^2/(2 + |t|)^2. The signed overlap equals t/(2 + |t|) for every unit vector u (signed-overlap-kernel-independence), so the run establishes that Garnet carries a relative pi phase on the signed branch through this compile, not a property of the sedenion kernel. Coherent gate errors add an odd term proportional to sqrt(|t|/(2 + |t|)) (iqm-garnet-signed-probe-coherent-response), so the single-visibility fit does not separate the linear law from that term: the measured ratio odd(0.05)/odd(0.25) = 0.26 +- 0.16 lies between the linear 0.22 and the amplitude scaling 0.47. The strong job lists its +t circuit before its -t circuit and the weak job its five +t circuits before its five -t circuits, so a reading that depends on batch position enters the odd part: a device with no signed response and a -0.044 shift on the second circuit reproduces the strong arm's +0.022, and the sign at |t| = 0.25 is resolved only under the assumption that position does not move the reading.
  Evidence: [signed_kernel_probe_analysis.json](../../results/iqm/signed_kernel_probe_analysis.json), [prediction.json](../../results/iqm/cases/signed-kernel-probe/prediction.json), [iqm-garnet-signed-kernel-probe.md](../design/iqm-garnet-signed-kernel-probe.md), [signed_kernel_probe.py](../../gororoba_quantum/signed_kernel_probe.py).
  Gates: replayed by core-637; source regenerated by core-638.
  Acquisitions: iqm-garnet-signed-kernel-probe-strong, iqm-garnet-signed-kernel-probe-weak.
  Depends on: iqm-garnet-reset-billing-pilot.
  Superseded by: iqm-garnet-signed-null-retest.
- **proposed-kernel-cancellation-probe** (proposed experiment, hardware). On Garnet the kernel cancellation probe (37 CZ on QB6, QB7, QB11, QB12 and QB17, calibration set 68b0f1d0) reads the control's <Z> as -V sin(2 phi) for the certified sedenion kernel vector around the balanced selector theta = pi/4 + phi and as V for the bright vector (e4 + e15)/sqrt2, so the odd part at phi = 0.1 and 0.3 carries the zero-divisor sign (the eigenvalue -1 of L_e1^T L_e10) while the bright odd part stays zero. The calibration model (exact, herald-conditioned readout) predicts visibility 0.48, kernel odd parts -0.0945 and -0.269, and a kernel even part of +0.052 from idle relaxation and readout; QB11's over-rotation, measured by prx trains of 1 to 13 pulses in the same job and fitted as -sin(N pi/2 eps), adds the noise model's -0.75 eps to that even part. Falsifiers: a kernel odd part three standard errors above zero (H1), a bright odd part three from the modeled bright odd part of idle relaxation (H2), the contrast odd(0.3) - (sin(0.6)/sin(0.2)) odd(0.1) three from zero (H3), the kernel even part three from its decomposition (H4); both kernel contrasts list their arms +, -, -, +.
  Boundary: cd_core settles the zero-divisor property exactly, so a resolved H1 demonstrates that the device realizes the compiled sign table at first order rather than discovering an algebraic fact; the open questions are device physics (QB11's coherent error, the even-part decomposition, the response shape). No approved hardware case; the prediction carries no coherent error, and H4 rests on the model's relaxation even part and on QB11's over-rotation being the only first-order term of this compile under the audit's stationary error model; an angle-dependent QB11 error rescales the odd part by twice what its trains predict and leaves its sign.
  Evidence: [iqm-garnet-kernel-cancellation-probe.md](../design/iqm-garnet-kernel-cancellation-probe.md), [kernel_cancellation.py](../../gororoba_quantum/kernel_cancellation.py), [iqm_cancellation_probe.py](../../scripts/iqm_cancellation_probe.py).
  Gates: source regenerated by core-642; active code regression checked by core-641.
  Related claims: signed-overlap-kernel-independence, iqm-garnet-signed-probe-coherent-response.
  Superseded by: iqm-garnet-kernel-cancellation.
- **proposed-signed-null-retest** (proposed experiment, hardware). On Garnet (calibration set 68b0f1d0, the retained 199-CZ compile) the null-controlled re-test reads D(0.25) = odd(0.25) - odd(0) over two +, -, -, + blocks at |t| = 0.25 and at t = 0: the retained +0.022 at |t| = 0.25 carries the sign of t only if D(0.25) lies three standard errors above zero. The calibration model (exact, herald-conditioned readout) predicts an odd part of +0.0136 at t = 0 and D(0.25) = +0.0095 (+0.0059 with a 2.7 % QB11 over-rotation), so a model-like device leaves D(0.25) unresolved at the planned standard error of 0.0047, while the retained visibility of 0.206 puts it near 4.8 standard errors. A |t| = 0.05 resolve sized at that visibility took 16 blocks (ceiling 43.5 credits); at the visibility the re-test then measured (0.058) it needs about 202 blocks and is not built.
  Boundary: No approved hardware case; the observable is kernel-independent (signed-overlap-kernel-independence), so the re-test measures the device's carried phase and its null offset, not the sedenion kernel.
  Evidence: [iqm-garnet-signed-kernel-probe.md](../design/iqm-garnet-signed-kernel-probe.md), [iqm_signed_resolve.py](../../scripts/iqm_signed_resolve.py).
  Gates: source regenerated by core-644; active code regression checked by core-643.
  Related claims: iqm-garnet-signed-kernel-probe, signed-overlap-kernel-independence.
  Superseded by: iqm-garnet-signed-null-retest.

## Withdrawn claims

- **withdrawn-ccz-resource-gain** (withdrawn claim, implementation). The CCZ normal form cuts the associator circuit by 18.9 percent of its CZ gates, raises fidelity 1.19x, and is a verified drop-in replacement for the factorized circuit.
  Boundary: The counts were typed-in estimates, the equivalence test compared CCZ with itself and a singular U_A, and a diagonal built from the predicted phase cannot replace the circuit that tests composition.
  Superseded by: associator-normal-form-compile.
  Withdrawn text: docs/evidence/ccz-reduction/ccz-optimization.ipynb and factorized-vs-ccz.ipynb at commit fa6783e, and docs/handoff-notes-2026-10-15.md at commit 900461a.
- **withdrawn-idle-dephasing-drift** (withdrawn claim, hardware). The -1.130 rad coherence rotation is a dephasing drift that dynamical decoupling mitigates.
  Boundary: A rotation is not a dephasing, and no DD acquisition exists.
  Superseded by: hw-coherence-idle-rotation.
  Withdrawn text: docs/notes/heron_spatial_multiprogramming.md before commit fd6832c.
- **withdrawn-modal-stationarity** (withdrawn claim, hardware). Every rank sector is stationary across the shot order.
  Boundary: The modal-outcome block statistic it rested on is blind to a distribution change that keeps the modal rate: p_first = (0.625, 0.375, 0) against p_last = (0.625, 0, 0.375) has TV distance 0.375.

  Withdrawn text: results/live/rank-sector-endpoint-decomposition.json and QPU_PROGRAM_CASES.md at commit 9baac24.
- **withdrawn-eightfold-cz-error** (withdrawn claim, hardware). The hardware's true CZ error is about eight times the calibrated 0.25 percent.

  Superseded by: hw-walk-scaffold-floor.
  Withdrawn text: docs/evidence/walk-control-scaffolding/README.md at commit 9476dc2.
- **withdrawn-unital-angle-argument** (withdrawn claim, hardware). Angle-dependent coherent errors cannot create the box-kite contrast because coherent errors are unital.
